Reads `stop_signal` from `finalize.json` because Step 5 + Step 6 propagate it there.\n   - `_record_maker_stop(state, plan_dir, defense)`: writes a `meta.notes` entry and updates `directors_notes.json`'s last `passes[]` entry with `stop_requested=true` + `stop_defense=defense`.\n5. **Update existing tests** that exercise `_resolve_review_outcome()` (search `tests/` for direct calls) to pass a `plan_dir` argument — typically a `tmp_path` fixture.\n6. **Resolves FLAG-002 / issue_hints-2 / correctness-2 / callers / FLAG-004 (data-path consumer half).**\n\n### Step 12: Director's notes assembled at the aggregate boundary only (`megaplan/execute/core.py`, `megaplan/forms/directors_notes.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **Add** `megaplan/forms/directors_notes.py`:\n   - `update_directors_notes_at_aggregate(plan_dir, state, aggregate_payload, *, iteration, voice, fired_provocations) -> None`: appends a single `passes[]` entry derived from the aggregate payload's `task_updates[].stance` and `stop_signal` fields. Reads/writes `plan_dir/directors_notes.json` atomically. Uses `iteration` as an idempotency key — if a `passes[]` entry already exists with that iteration number, it is *replaced*, not appended. **(Resolves scope.)**\n2. **Hook** ONLY into `_build_aggregate_execution_payload()` at `megaplan/execute/core.py` (call sites at lines `431, 679, 1033`): when `is_creative_mode(state)`, call `update_directors_notes_at_aggregate(...)` once per execute run with the final aggregate. **Do NOT hook into `_merge_batch_results()`** — that function only propagates `stance` / `stop_signal` into `finalize.json` (Step 6) and never touches the sidecar.\n3. **Hook** into `handle_critique()` so the *initial* triad is recorded in `directors_notes.json` immediately after critique writes its output, even when execution hasn't run yet. Use the same idempotency-key-by-iteration semantics — re-running critique on the same iteration replaces, doesn't append.\n4. **Resolves scope-2 (single hook) and scope (idempotency).**\n\n### Step 13: Stance validation enforcement (`megaplan/execute/core.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **Update** `_merge_batch_results()` (`megaplan/execute/core.py`) to call `validate_stance(task_update[\"stance\"])` for each task update with a stance, when `is_creative_mode(state)`. Violations are stored on the task as `stance_violations: list[str]` (a new field added to `merge_fields` in Step 6 if needed; otherwise written through to the merged task record directly).\n2. **Bubble** violations into the next critique pass: the critique prompt builder (Step 9) reads `directors_notes.json` and, if the prior pass had `stance_violations`, prepends a stance-authenticity sub-provocation: \"Read it aloud. If it's safe, the work is too. Rewrite the part that would embarrass you to say to a stranger.\"\n3. **Soft enforcement only** (no hard reject).\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Tests + validation\n\n### Step 14: Pure-function unit tests (`tests/test_modes_helpers.py`, `tests/test_forms_registry.py`, `tests/test_provocations.py`, `tests/test_stance_validator.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **`tests/test_modes_helpers.py`**: `is_creative_mode` true for `mode=creative` and `mode=joke`; `creative_form_id` returns `\"joke\"` for legacy joke state with no form key, returns `state.config.form` for new state, returns `None` for code mode. `is_prose_mode` true for doc/joke/creative.\n2. **`tests/test_forms_registry.py`**: `get_form(\"joke\")` and `get_form(\"poem\")` exist; both have ≥3 cuts/forces/sparks; joke has 5 beat IDs, poem has 3; both declare `execution_schema_key == \"execution_doc.json\"`.\n3. **`tests/test_provocations.py`**: light=2, standard=3, robust=3 with rotating voice; `prior_provocation_ids` filters duplicates on standard iteration 2; `over_explained` tag biases selection.\n4. **`tests/test_stance_validator.py`**: valid passes; >50 words → violation; hedging verb → violation; third-person → violation; no claim marker → violation.\n\n### Step 15: Workflow + handler integration tests (`tests/test_handle_init_creative_mode.py`, `tests/test_workflow_creative.py`, `tests/test_review_stop_affordance.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **`tests/test_handle_init_creative_mode.py`**: `--mode creative --form joke --output …` succeeds and writes `state.config.form=\"joke\"`; `--mode creative` without `--form` raises `invalid_args`; `--mode creative --form poem --primary-criterion \"tightest image\"` accepted.\n2. **Update** `tests/test_handle_init_joke_mode.py`: `--mode joke` still works AND now writes `state.config.form=\"joke\"`.\n3. **`tests/test_workflow_creative.py`** — three explicit assertions:\n   - `workflow_next()` for creative-standard at `STATE_INITIALIZED` returns `prep` (not `plan`); code-standard returns `plan` (no regression).\n   - `workflow_next()` for creative-light at `STATE_INITIALIZED` returns `prep` (creative-light runs prep, unlike code-light); code-light returns `plan`.\n   - `workflow_next()` for creative-light at `STATE_PLANNED` returns `finalize` (skips critique); code-light returns `critique` (no regression). **(Resolves FLAG-005 / issue_hints test coverage.)**\n4. **`tests/test_review_stop_affordance.py`**: build a state with a needs_rework review and a `stop_signal.requested=true` task in finalize; assert `_resolve_review_outcome(plan_dir, …)` returns `(\"success\", STATE_DONE, None)` and `meta.notes` records the defense. Without the stop signal, the same input returns `(\"needs_rework\", STATE_FINALIZED, \"execute\")`. Code mode with stop_signal does NOT short-circuit. Also assert the resolver signature accepts `plan_dir` as the first positional argument. **(Resolves correctness-2 / callers test coverage.)**\n\n### Step 16: Schema + runtime-layout tests (`tests/test_runtime_layout_creative.py`, expand `tests/test_execute_merge_creative.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **`tests/test_runtime_layout_creative.py`**: call `ensure_runtime_layout()`; assert `.megaplan/schemas/execution_doc.json` exists and includes `stance` + `stop_signal` properties; assert `.megaplan/schemas/finalize.json` exists and includes `stance` + `stop_signal` on `tasks[]`; assert `.megaplan/schemas/directors_notes.json` exists; assert `get_execution_schema_key(\"creative\", \"poem\") == \"execution_doc.json\"`.\n2. **`tests/test_execute_merge_creative.py`**: build a synthetic execution_doc payload with a `task_update` containing `stance` + `stop_signal`; call `_merge_batch_results(..., mode=\"creative\")`; assert both fields land on the corresponding task in `finalize_data[\"tasks\"]`. **(Resolves FLAG-004 / correctness-1 test coverage.)**\n\n### Step 17: Prompt builder tests (`tests/test_prompts_creative.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **`tests/test_prompts_creative.py`**:\n   - `_critique_creative_prompt(form=joke, robustness=\"standard\")` mentions exactly 1 cut, 1 force, 1 spark by name.\n   - `_critique_creative_prompt(form=poem, ...)` references poem beats, not screenplay beats.\n   - `_execute_creative_prompt` includes stance + stop-affordance instructions.\n   - Code-mode `_execute_prompt` does NOT include stop-affordance instructions.\n   - Iteration 2 critique excludes provocation IDs from a fixture `directors_notes.json`.\n   - Dispatcher: `create_claude_prompt(\"critique\", state_with_mode_joke, …)` ≡ `create_claude_prompt(\"critique\", state_with_mode_creative_form_joke, …)`.\n   - `select_active_checks(state, robustness)` returns the same output as `joke_checks_for_robustness(robustness)` for joke (back-compat).\n\n### Step 18: End-to-end smoke + idempotency (`tests/test_creative_mode_smoke.py`, `tests/test_directors_notes_idempotency.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **`tests/test_creative_mode_smoke.py`**:\n   - `--mode creative --form poem --output poems/p.md` → prep+plan+critique+revise+execute+review.\n   - Mocked execute returns a valid stance + `stop_signal.requested=false`. Assert `directors_notes.json` is written exactly once per execute run, contains form, primary_criterion, fired provocations, stances.\n   - Second run: mocked execute returns `stop_signal.requested=true`, mocked review returns `needs_rework`. Assert review halts with STATE_DONE and `meta.notes` records the defense.\n2. **`tests/test_directors_notes_idempotency.py`**: simulate a multi-batch execute; assert `directors_notes.json` has exactly ONE `passes[]` entry per iteration (not one per batch). Re-run aggregate assembly with the same iteration; assert the entry is replaced, not duplicated. **(Resolves scope test coverage.)**\n\n### Step 19: Run focused tests, then full suite\n**Scope:** Small\n\n1. **Run** unit tests (Steps 14, 16) — fastest, no fixtures.\n2. **Run** init/workflow/review tests (Step 15).\n3. **Run** prompt builder tests (Step 17).\n4. **Run** smoke + idempotency (Step 18) and existing `tests/test_joke_mode_smoke.py`.\n5. **Run** full `pytest tests/` to catch regressions, especially anywhere a `mode in {\"doc\", \"joke\"}` site was rewritten or `_resolve_review_outcome()` is called.\n\n---\n\n## Execution Order\n\n1. **Phase 1** (helpers + registry + schemas + workflow) lands first.\n2. Inside Phase 1: Step 1 (helpers) → Step 2 (forms registry) → Step 3 (init/CLI) → Step 4 (workflow override) → Step 5 (schemas + runtime layout).\n3. **Phase 2** (mode-aware sweep + merge_fields extension) is mechanical and runs after Phase 1.\n4. **Phase 3** (provocation engine + stance + stop-affordance + sidecar) depends on Steps 1, 2, 6 being done.\n5. **Phase 4 tests** can begin in parallel with Phase 3 once Steps 1-8 are committed.\n6. The riskiest moment is Step 6 (mode sweep + merge_fields); land it green before starting Phase 3. Step 11 (resolver signature change) should also land before Step 12 so smoke tests can exercise the full halt path.\n\n## Validation Order\n\n1. Pure-function unit tests (helpers, registry, selection, stance validator).\n2. Schema + runtime-layout tests + merge-fields propagation test.\n3. Workflow + handler tests (including the three new workflow_next assertions and the resolver signature assertion).\n4. Prompt builder tests.\n5. End-to-end smoke + idempotency tests with mocked agents.\n6. Full pytest suite.",

    "Adding `stop_signal` to `merge_fields` and to `finalize.json` `tasks[]` is the right place for the stop-signal source of truth.     "`_resolve_review_outcome()` takes `plan_dir` as the first positional parameter (not a kwarg) because both verified call sites can pass it positionally without ambiguity, and adding kwargs to a function with this many positional args invites future bugs.     "For creative-light at STATE_PLANNED, the chosen override is `Transition('finalize', STATE_GATED)` so the workflow goes plan → finalize → STATE_FINALIZED → execute → STATE_EXECUTED → (light-overridden empty) → done.     "Director's notes uses `iteration` (not `(iteration, batch_index)`) as the idempotency key because Step 12 hooks only at the aggregate boundary, not per-batch. One pass entry per iteration matches the brief's 'pass' semantics.",
    "The v2 plan's claim that prep stays in STATE_INITIALIZED was wrong; verified `WORKFLOW[STATE_INITIALIZED] = [Transition('prep', STATE_PREPPED)]`. Step 4 is now correct.",
